99536					CONF							0	0	0	0	true		0	0												0	0	0	0	0												Transfer of chargeoffs from pushdown accounting to reserve for loans related to preacquisition.		One Bank of Tennessee (via BPC Corporation, holding company) purchased Citizens First Bank in January 2019. CFB merged with and into OBT in October 2019. The acquisition resulted in a push down accounting adjustment. The push down accounting is analyzed at the end of the 4th quarter annually and adjusted based upon the outstanding balances of the original loans acquired at acquisition date. The adjustment to report RI-B.II reflects a transfer from push down accounting reserves to actual reserves for charge-offs related to the acquisition of CFB and a prorated reduction of the push down accounting balance resulting from paid out loans. The process was reviewed and approved by FDIC Examiners February 2021.
